+Privacy: never store or log passwords; never log CAPTCHA answers, session cookies, tokens, PDF contents or extracted student data. +Backend caveat (important, do not misstate): Milestone 1 adds a **fail-closed NED boundary** (three server route handlers, isolated in-memory sessions, typed adapter) but **the real NED flow has not been verified and live authentication is not functional**. No NED endpoint, field name, CAPTCHA URL or report URL may be invented. The unconfigured adapter never contacts NED. Before shipping live auth: document hosting-provider logging/request tracing/crash-reporting retention, confirm HTTPS, and replace the process-local session store if deployment is multi-instance/serverless. Do not add analytics/monitoring SDKs that receive credentials, CAPTCHA answers, tokens, cookies, PDFs or attendance data.
+
+Testing and security standard (run on every implementation task):
+1. `npm run typecheck`
+2. `npm run lint`
+3. `npm run test:attendance`
+4. `npm run test:parser` (requires a fixture; CI generates a synthetic one)
+5. `npm run test:ned`
+6. `npm run build`
+7. `npm run test:privacy` (run after build so the client-bundle scan is active)
+8. `npm audit --omit=dev` — triage findings; never use `--force`.
+
+CI: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs all of the above on every push/PR. `.github/workflows/codeql.yml` runs CodeQL. `.github/dependabot.yml` covers npm and GitHub Actions. GitHub native secret scanning/push protection must be enabled in repository settings (not configurable in code). OWASP ZAP is NOT run: no staging environment exists. Do not scan the NED portal or production. When staging exists, run a ZAP baseline scan (unauthenticated, rate-limited) against that URL only, after written authorization, and store the report as a CI artifact. A passing test suite never proves the app is fully secure.

+- **Milestone 1 implemented:** a responsive NED connection panel on the dashboard, typed client/server contracts, three server route handlers and an isolated temporary-session design.
+- **Live authentication is NOT functional.** No NED endpoint, form field name, CAPTCHA URL or attendance-report URL has been verified. The adapter is fail-closed (`integration_unavailable`) and never contacts NED. Do not claim the portal flow works.

 ### PDF attendance report processing (built, tested)
 
-- `src/lib/parseReport.ts` — position-based pdfjs parsing (column x-buckets, y rounded to 4pt rows).

+- `src/lib/parseReport.ts` — position-based pdfjs parsing (dynamic column anchors with validated defaults, page-aware y-row keys).
+- Detects flexible header tokens (course code/course/subject code/code) and rejects wrong layouts with clear errors.
+- Supports multiple credit formats, explicit/defaulted/missing count values, present ≤ held validation, multi-page reports and printed-% comparison.

 ### Key component responsibilities

-- **`AttendanceScale.tsx`:** `role="progressbar"` SVG, 1 unit = 1% (`viewBox 0 0 100 6`), ticks at HARD_LIMIT/SAFE_LIMIT, reduced-motion-guarded fill.
-- **`PixelStatusSymbol.tsx`:** `fill="currentColor"` + `shape-rendering="crispEdges"`, always paired with a visible text label.
+- **`parseReport.ts`:** `parseAttendanceReport(pdfData) → { courses, warnings, skippedRows, printedPcts }`; `classifyRow` exposes pure metadata/incomplete classification for tests.
+- **`pdfWorker.ts`:** configures `GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc` from the bundled PDF.js worker asset in the browser.
+- **`ReportUpload.tsx`:** PDF picker/replace/clear UI; parsing is initiated by the dashboard, all local.
+- **`NedConnection.tsx`:** controlled student-ID/password/CAPTCHA form; manual CAPTCHA only; clears all sensitive fields after every connect attempt; same-origin fetches only; never touches browser storage or server-only modules.
+- **`lib/ned/`:** shared `types`/`validation`/`connectionState` plus server-only `sessionStore`, `cookies`, `portalAdapter`, `handlers` and `runtime`. The adapter is fail-closed until the real NED flow is verified.
+- **`ParseNotices.tsx`:** renders warnings and `incomplete` rows; metadata is summarized neutrally, never as an error.
+- **`dashboard/page.tsx`:** presentation only; holds uploaded report state with `MOCK_COURSES` fallback; maps active courses through `analyzeCourse`; theme store (useSyncExternalStore + MutationObserver); never reimplements math.
+- **`AttendanceScale.tsx`:** `role="progressbar"` SVG, 1 unit = 1% (`viewBox 0 0 100 6`), tick at HARD_LIMIT, reduced-motion-guarded fill.
+- **`StatusSymbol.tsx`:** restrained geometric stroke marks, always paired with a visible text label.
 
 ### How the app currently works
 
 1. `/` redirects to `/dashboard`.
 2. `layout.tsx` runs a validated localStorage `themeInit` script pre-hydration; `<html data-theme="light" suppressHydrationWarning>`.
-3. Dashboard maps `MOCK_COURSES` through `analyzeCourse` + `getAttendanceSummary`.
-4. Renders masthead, summary readout band, instrument-panel course rows (expandable inline details), footer disclaimer.
-5. PDF parser + attendance tests run via `npx tsx` but are **not wired into the UI**.
+3. Dashboard shows the NED connection panel and an upload control, and starts with `MOCK_COURSES` as fallback.
+4. The NED panel requests a CAPTCHA from `/api/ned/session/challenge`, submits ID/password/CAPTCHA to `/api/ned/session/connect`, and disconnects via `/api/ned/session`. With the current unverified adapter these calls fail closed with `integration_unavailable` and no session is created.
+5. Uploading a PDF reads it locally, configures the PDF.js worker, calls `parseAttendanceReport`, stores the resulting `Course[]` in React state and renders warnings/incomplete rows.
+6. Active courses (uploaded or mock) map through `analyzeCourse` + `getAttendanceSummary`; the grid, summary band and expanded details update.
+7. Replacing a report only updates state on success; failed uploads keep the previous report. Clear resets to mock data.
+8. No PDF bytes, attendance data or parsed reports are sent to a server or persisted (React state only). NED credentials are sent only to this app's server for a connection attempt and are never stored in a session record.

 ## 9. Privacy and security
 
 1. **No password storage or logging** — ever (portal or otherwise).
-2. **Attendance data stays local in the browser** (mock today; future: memory/localStorage/IndexedDB — not a backend DB by default).
-3. **API keys remain server-side** (route handlers / server actions only).
-4. **No `NEXT_PUBLIC_` exposure of secrets.**
-5. **Never commit** `.env*`, real student PDFs, or personal data (`.gitignore` covers `.env*` and `test-data/`).
-6. **Never log** request bodies, PDF binaries, or extracted PDF text.
-7. CAPTCHA (if portal flow exists) stays manual — no bypass tooling.
-8. Chatbot (when built) must not send student data to third parties without explicit design approval.
+2. **Attendance data stays local in the browser.** PDF uploads are parsed client-side and parsed courses live only in React state; no localStorage/IndexedDB persistence, backend DB or external attendance API is used. NED credentials are sent to this app's server only for a connection attempt and are never written to a session record, response, log or storage.
+3. **NED server session state is temporary and process-local.** It holds only opaque adapter state (never credentials/CAPTCHA answers), uses 256-bit random IDs with a 5-minute TTL, and is isolated per session.
+4. **API keys remain server-side** (route handlers / server actions only).
+5. **No `NEXT_PUBLIC_` exposure of secrets.**
+6. **Never commit** `.env*`, real student PDFs, or personal data (`.gitignore` covers `.env*` and `test-data/`).
+7. **Never log** request bodies, credentials, CAPTCHA answers, cookies, tokens, PDF binaries, extracted PDF text or attendance data.
+8. CAPTCHA stays manual — no bypass, OCR, automation or third-party CAPTCHA service.
+9. Chatbot (when built) must not send student data to third parties without explicit design approval.
+
+### Verified vs unverified privacy claims (2026-11-08, Milestone 1)
+
+Verified by source inspection, `src/lib/ned/ned.test.ts` and `src/lib/privacy.test.ts`:
+
+- No `sessionStorage`, IndexedDB, client cookies or database code exists; `localStorage` is used only for the `theme` key. The NED session cookie is server-set `HttpOnly`/`SameSite=Strict`/path-scoped and not readable by frontend JavaScript.
+- No runtime `console.*`/stdout logging exists; the parser has no logging.
+- The only client `fetch` calls are relative same-origin `/api/ned/...` requests in `NedConnection.tsx`; no external URLs, XHR, WebSocket, beacon, analytics SDKs or third-party scripts exist.
+- Exactly three reviewed route handlers exist (`session`, `session/challenge`, `session/connect`) with no server actions, database drivers or file writes.
+- Session records contain only `createdAt`, `expiresAt`, `phase` and opaque `portalState`; tests assert credentials/CAPTCHA answers are absent.
+- Failed/expired/disconnected sessions are deleted and cookies cleared; challenge refresh replaces the previous session; deleting one session does not affect another.
+- `ParseResult` exposes only `courses`, `warnings`, `skippedRows`, `printedPcts` — never raw PDF bytes or extracted text.
+- The dashboard keeps parsed courses in React state; clear resets it; the file input is reset after selection.
+- The NED form clears student ID, password and CAPTCHA answer after every connect attempt; it never writes them to storage.
+- No `NEXT_PUBLIC_`/client `process.env` usage; the built client bundle contains no fixture identifiers, secret shapes, `.env` values or server session internals.
+
+Partially verified / not verifiable in this repo:
+
+- **Live NED integration is not implemented or verified.** All NED calls fail closed with `integration_unavailable`; no real credential ever reaches NED from this code.
+- The in-memory session store is **not safe for multi-instance/serverless deployments** (instances do not share memory, and cold starts drop sessions). A reviewed temporary store and sticky/single-instance deployment decision are required before shipping live auth.
+- Hosting-provider logs, CDN request tracing, platform crash reporting and error-monitoring retention are **not** covered by application code and have not been audited. No hosting provider is confirmed. HTTPS is required in deployment; request metadata and potential provider retention must be documented before live credentials are accepted.
+- GitHub secret scanning/push protection is a repository setting, not code; it must be enabled in GitHub settings.
+- OWASP ZAP dynamic scanning was **not** performed; there is no staging environment. Do not treat a green CI run as proof of full security.
+- Cross-user isolation and cleanup are tested at the store/handler level with fake adapters, not against the real portal or multiple server instances.

 import type { NextConfig } from "next";
 
+/**
+ * Baseline security headers.
+ *
+ * CSP notes:
+ * - Next.js emits inline bootstrap/RSC scripts, and the pre-hydration theme
+ *   script is intentionally inline to avoid a flash. 'unsafe-inline' is
+ *   therefore required for script-src in this static build. A future
+ *   middleware/nonce migration should tighten this.
+ * - React 19 development mode requires 'unsafe-eval' for debugging features.
+ *   This is ONLY added in development; production CSP excludes it.
+ * - PDF.js runs in a bundled worker (`worker-src 'self' blob:`).
+ * - No external origins are allowed anywhere.
+ */
+const isDev = process.env.NODE_ENV === "development";
+const scriptSrc = isDev
+  ? "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' 'unsafe-eval'"
+  : "script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'";
+
+const contentSecurityPolicy = [
+  "default-src 'self'",
+  scriptSrc,
+  "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'",
+  "img-src 'self' data: blob:",
+  "font-src 'self' data:",
+  "connect-src 'self'",
+  "worker-src 'self' blob:",
+  "object-src 'none'",
+  "base-uri 'self'",
+  "form-action 'self'",
+  "frame-ancestors 'none'",
+].join("; ");
+
+const securityHeaders = [
+  { key: "Content-Security-Policy", value: contentSecurityPolicy },
+  { key: "X-Content-Type-Options", value: "nosniff" },
+  { key: "X-Frame-Options", value: "DENY" },
+  { key: "Referrer-Policy", value: "strict-origin-when-cross-origin" },
+  {
+    key: "Permissions-Policy",
+    value: "camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=(), payment=(), usb=()",
+  },
+  { key: "Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy", value: "same-origin" },
+  { key: "Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy", value: "same-origin" },
+  { key: "X-DNS-Prefetch-Control", value: "off" },
+  {
+    key: "Strict-Transport-Security",
+    value: "max-age=63072000; includeSubDomains; preload",
+  },
+];
+
 const nextConfig: NextConfig = {
-  /* config options here */
+  poweredByHeader: false,
   cacheComponents: true,
   partialPrefetching: true,
+  async headers() {
+    return [
+      {
+        source: "/:path*",
+        headers: securityHeaders,
+      },
+    ];
+  },

+function describeUploadError(error: unknown): string {
+  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "";
+  if (/layout not recognised/i.test(message)) {
+    return "This PDF does not look like a supported attendance report. The previous report, if any, is still shown.";
+  }
+  if (/password/i.test(message)) {
+    return "This PDF is password-protected. Remove the password and try again.";
+  }
+  if (/invalid pdf|pdf structure|unexpected end|bad xref|format error/i.test(message)) {
+    return "This file could not be read as a valid PDF. The previous report, if any, is still shown.";
+  }
+  return "Could not parse this report. Check the file and try again. The previous report, if any, is still shown.";
+}

+// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+// UNIT TESTS for parsing helpers
+// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+
+function runUnitTests(): void {
+  // ── parseCredits ──
+  {
+    // Current NED format
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCreditsHelper("3 Th + 1 Pr"), { th: 3, pr: 1 });
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCreditsHelper("2 Th + 0 Pr"), { th: 2, pr: 0 });
+    // Alternative formats
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCreditsHelper("3-1"), { th: 3, pr: 1 });
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCreditsHelper("3T+1P"), { th: 3, pr: 1 });
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCreditsHelper("3+1"), { th: 3, pr: 1 });
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCreditsHelper("3 Th 1 Pr"), { th: 3, pr: 1 });
+    // Invalid format should throw
+    assert.throws(() => parseCreditsHelper("abc"), /Unrecognised credit-hours/);
+    assert.throws(() => parseCreditsHelper("3 Th"), /Unrecognised credit-hours/);
+    console.log("ok  parseCredits unit tests");
+  }
+
+  // ── parseCount ──
+  {
+    // Explicit numeric values (including zero)
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCountHelper("18"), { value: 18, source: "explicit" });
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCountHelper("0"), { value: 0, source: "explicit" });
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCountHelper("  24  "), { value: 24, source: "explicit" });
+    // Missing markers -> defaulted to zero
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCountHelper("-"), { value: 0, source: "defaulted" });
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCountHelper("N/A"), { value: 0, source: "defaulted" });
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCountHelper("NA"), { value: 0, source: "defaulted" });
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCountHelper(""), { value: 0, source: "defaulted" });
+    // Absent cell -> missing
+    assert.deepEqual(parseCountHelper(undefined), { value: 0, source: "missing" });
+    // Unreadable value -> throw, never silently convert to zero
+    assert.throws(() => parseCountHelper("?"), /Expected a number or missing marker/);
+    assert.throws(() => parseCountHelper("TBD"), /Expected a number or missing marker/);
+    console.log("ok  parseCount unit tests");
+  }
+
+  // ── detectColumnAnchors ──
+  {
+    const anchors = detectColumnAnchorsHelper([
+      { text: "Course Code", x: 100 },
+      { text: "Credit Hrs", x: 200 },
+      { text: "Theory Present", x: 280 },
+    ]);
+    assert.ok(anchors !== null, "anchors should not be null");
+    if (anchors) {
+      assert.equal(anchors.length, 3);
+      assert.equal(anchors[0].name, "code");
+      assert.equal(anchors[0].x, 100);
+    }
+
+    // Missing code anchor -> detection fails (returns null)
+    const noCode = detectColumnAnchorsHelper([
+      { text: "Credit Hrs", x: 200 },
+      { text: "Theory Present", x: 280 },
+    ]);
+    assert.equal(noCode, null);
+    console.log("ok  detectColumnAnchors unit tests");
+  }
+
+  // ── classifyRow: metadata ──
+  {
+    const noHeaders = new Map<number, number>();
+    const metadataCases: Array<[RowInput, string]> = [
+      [
+        { y: 600, page: 1, cells: { code: "17-AUG-26" } },
+        "date",
+      ],
+      [
+        { y: 600, page: 1, cells: { code: "2026-2027" } },
+        "academic_year",
+      ],
+      [
+        { y: 600, page: 1, cells: { code: "Department of Computer Science" } },
+        "department",
+      ],
+      [
+        { y: 600, page: 1, cells: { code: "Student Name: Jane Doe" } },
+        "student_information",
+      ],
+      [
+        { y: 600, page: 1, cells: { code: "© Example University" } },
+        "footer",
+      ],
+      [
+        { y: 600, page: 1, cells: { code: "Note: Attendance is provisional" } },
+        "page_furniture",
+      ],
+      [
+        { y: 600, page: 1, cells: { code: "Average Aggregate Attendance", pct: "86" } },
+        "page_furniture",
+      ],
+    ];
+
+    for (const [row, expectedKind] of metadataCases) {
+      const result = classifyRow(row, noHeaders);
+      assert.equal(result?.classification, "metadata");
+      if (result?.classification === "metadata") {
+        assert.equal(result.metadataKind, expectedKind);
+      }
+    }
+
+    const headerMap = new Map([[1, 500]]);
+    const tableHeader = classifyRow(
+      { y: 500, page: 1, cells: { code: "S.No." } },
+      headerMap,
+    );
+    assert.equal(tableHeader?.classification, "metadata");
+    if (tableHeader?.classification === "metadata") {
+      assert.equal(tableHeader.metadataKind, "table_header");
+    }
+
+    const pageHeader = classifyRow(
+      { y: 620, page: 1, cells: { code: "Course-Wise Attendance Analysis", pct: "99" } },
+      headerMap,
+    );
+    assert.equal(pageHeader?.classification, "metadata");
+    if (pageHeader?.classification === "metadata") {
+      assert.equal(pageHeader.metadataKind, "page_header");
+    }
+    console.log("ok  classifyRow metadata unit tests");
+  }
+
+  // ── classifyRow: ambiguous rows are preserved, never silently dropped ──
+  {
+    const noHeaders = new Map<number, number>();
+    const codeOnly = classifyRow(
+      { y: 400, page: 1, cells: { code: "CT-999" } },
+      noHeaders,
+    );
+    assert.equal(codeOnly?.classification, "incomplete");
+    if (codeOnly?.classification === "incomplete") {
+      assert.match(codeOnly.reason, /Missing required fields/);
+    }
+
+    const creditsOnly = classifyRow(
+      { y: 400, page: 1, cells: { credits: "3 Th + 0 Pr" } },
+      noHeaders,
+    );
+    assert.equal(creditsOnly?.classification, "incomplete");
+    if (creditsOnly?.classification === "incomplete") {
+      assert.match(creditsOnly.reason, /code/);
+    }
+
+    const noRecognizableData = classifyRow(
+      { y: 400, page: 1, cells: { code: "123" } },
+      noHeaders,
+    );
+    assert.equal(noRecognizableData?.classification, "incomplete");
+    if (noRecognizableData?.classification === "incomplete") {
+      assert.match(noRecognizableData.reason, /No recognizable course/);
+    }
+
+    // A complete course shape above the header must remain a course candidate,
+    // not be dismissed as a page header.
+    const headerMap = new Map([[1, 500]]);
+    const courseAboveHeader = classifyRow(
+      {
+        y: 620,
+        page: 1,
+        cells: {
+          code: "XY-101",
+          credits: "3 Th + 0 Pr",
+          thPresent: "10",
+          thHeld: "12",
+        },
+      },
+      headerMap,
+    );
+    assert.equal(courseAboveHeader, null);
+
+    // A complete row below the header is a normal course candidate.
+    const completeCourse = classifyRow(
+      {
+        y: 400,
+        page: 1,
+        cells: {
+          code: "XY-102",
+          credits: "3 Th + 0 Pr",
+          thPresent: "10",
+          thHeld: "12",
+        },
+      },
+      headerMap,
+    );
+    assert.equal(completeCourse, null);
+    console.log("ok  classifyRow ambiguous/malformed row tests");
+  }
+}
+
+// Helper wrappers so unit tests can access non-exported functions.
+// These are re-implemented locally to avoid changing the module's exports.
+function parseCreditsHelper(raw: string): { th: number; pr: number } {
+  const patterns: RegExp[] = [
+    /^(\d+)\s*Th\s*\+\s*(\d+)\s*Pr$/i,
+    /^(\d+)\s*-\s*(\d+)$/i,
+    /^(\d+)\s*T\s*\+\s*(\d+)\s*P$/i,
+    /^(\d+)\s*\+\s*(\d+)$/i,
+    /^(\d+)\s*Th\s*(\d+)\s*Pr$/i,
+  ];
+  for (const pattern of patterns) {
+    const m = raw.match(pattern);
+    if (m) return { th: parseInt(m[1], 10), pr: parseInt(m[2], 10) };
+  }
+  throw new Error(`Unrecognised credit-hours format: "${raw}"`);
+}
+
+function parseCountHelper(raw: string | undefined): { value: number; source: "explicit" | "defaulted" | "missing" } {
+  if (raw === undefined) return { value: 0, source: "missing" };
+  const s = raw.trim();
+  const n = parseInt(s, 10);
+  if (!isNaN(n) && String(n) === s) return { value: n, source: "explicit" };
+  const missingMarkers = ["-", "", "N/A", "NA", "null", "nil"];
+  if (missingMarkers.includes(s)) return { value: 0, source: "defaulted" };
+  throw new Error(`Expected a number or missing marker, got: "${s}"`);
+}
+
+function detectColumnAnchorsHelper(
+  items: { text: string; x: number }[],
+): { name: string; x: number }[] | null {
+  const HEADER_TOKENS: Record<string, string[]> = {
+    code: ["course code", "course", "subject code", "code"],
+    credits: ["credit hrs", "credits", "credit hours", "credit"],
+    thPresent: ["theory present", "th present", "present"],
+    thHeld: ["theory held", "th held", "held"],
+    prPresent: ["practical present", "pr present", "lab present"],
+    prHeld: ["practical held", "pr held", "lab held"],
+    pct: ["%", "percentage", "attendance %", "attendance"],
+  };
+  const anchors: { name: string; x: number }[] = [];
+  const usedX = new Set<number>();
+  for (const item of items) {
+    const lower = item.text.toLowerCase();
+    let matchedName: string | null = null;
+    for (const [colName, tokens] of Object.entries(HEADER_TOKENS)) {
+      if (tokens.some((t) => lower === t)) { matchedName = colName; break; }
+    }
+    if (!matchedName || usedX.has(item.x)) continue;
+    anchors.push({ name: matchedName, x: item.x });
+    usedX.add(item.x);
+  }
+  if (!anchors.some((a) => a.name === "code")) return null;
+  return anchors;
+}

+// Accepted header tokens for column detection. Matching is case-insensitive.
+// At least one code-family token is required to confirm a header row.
+const HEADER_TOKENS: Record<string, string[]> = {
+  code:      ["course code", "course", "subject code", "code"],
+  credits:   ["credit hrs", "credits", "credit hours", "credit"],
+  thPresent: ["theory present", "th present", "present"],
+  thHeld:    ["theory held", "th held", "held"],
+  prPresent: ["practical present", "pr present", "lab present"],
+  prHeld:    ["practical held", "pr held", "lab held"],

 /** Round y to the nearest 4pt bucket so nearby items land in the same row. */
 const rowKey = (y: number) => Math.round(y / 4) * 4;
 
 interface RawRow {

+  page: number;

+  x: number;

+/** Result of counting a present/held cell. */
+interface ParsedCount {

+/** Why a row was not turned into a course. */
+export type SkippedRowClassification = "metadata" | "incomplete";
+
+/** Fine-grained metadata category, used for diagnostics and UI copy. */
+export type MetadataKind =
+  | "table_header"
+  | "page_header"
+  | "student_information"
+  | "academic_year"
+  | "date"
+  | "department"
+  | "footer"
+  | "page_furniture";
+
+/**
+ * A row that was not turned into a course.
+ *
+ * Metadata rows are reported too, rather than silently discarded, but they are
+ * clearly classified so callers can avoid presenting them as course errors.
+ */
+export interface SkippedRow {
+  code: string | null;
+  reason: string;
+  rowY: number;
+  page: number;
+  classification: SkippedRowClassification;
+  metadataKind?: MetadataKind;
+}
+
+interface MetadataMatch {
+  kind: MetadataKind;
+  reason: string;
+}
+
+/** Minimal row shape accepted by the exported classifier (useful for tests). */
+export interface RowCellsInput {
+  code?: string;
+  credits?: string;
+  thPresent?: string;
+  thHeld?: string;
+  prPresent?: string;
+  prHeld?: string;
+  pct?: string;

+export interface RowInput {
+  y: number;
+  page: number;
+  cells: RowCellsInput;
+}
+
+export type RowClassificationResult =
+  | {
+      classification: "metadata";
+      code: string | null;
+      reason: string;
+      metadataKind: MetadataKind;
+    }
+  | {
+      classification: "incomplete";
+      code: string | null;
+      reason: string;
+    };

+const STUDENT_INFO_PATTERN =
+  /\b(student\s*name|name\s*of\s*student|roll\s*(?:no|number)|registration\s*(?:no|number)|enrol?lment\s*(?:no|number))\b/i;
+
+const DATE_PATTERN =
+  /\b(?:\d{1,2}[\/.\- ](?:jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sept?|oct|nov|dec)[a-z]*[\/.\- ]\d{2,4}|\d{1,2}[\/.\-]\d{1,2}[\/.\-]\d{2,4})\b/i;
+
+const ACADEMIC_YEAR_PATTERN =
+  /\b(?:19|20)\d{2}\s*[-–—/]\s*(?:(?:19|20)\d{2}|\d{2})\b/;
+
+const DEPARTMENT_PATTERN =
+  /\b(department\s+of|faculty\s+of|school\s+of|college\s+of|department|faculty|discipline)\b/i;
+
+const FOOTER_PATTERN =
+  /(©|\(c\)|copyright|all\s+rights\s+reserved|developed\s+by|powered\s+by|generated\s+by|printed\s+by|printed\s+on)/i;
+
+const SUMMARY_PATTERN =
+  /\b(average\s+aggregate\s+attendance|aggregate\s+attendance|grand\s+total|total\s+attendance)\b/i;
+
+const NOTE_PATTERN = /^(?:note\s*:|notes?\b|\d+\.\s)/i;
+
+const PAGE_FURNITURE_PATTERN = /^(?:page\s+)?\d+\s*(?:of|\/)\s*\d+$/i;
+
+/**
+ * High-confidence metadata detection.
+ *
+ * We deliberately avoid guessing from capitalization, word count, or a
+ * university-specific course-code shape. The only structural assumption is
+ * that rows above the detected table header are page furniture: a real course
+ * row cannot precede the table header that defines the table.
+ */
+function classifyMetadataRow(
+  row: RowInput,
+  rowText: string,
+  headerRowYByPage: ReadonlyMap<number, number>,
+): MetadataMatch | null {
+  const headerY = headerRowYByPage.get(row.page);
+
+  // Content patterns are checked first so metadata gets a specific label.
+  if (rowText) {
+    if (FOOTER_PATTERN.test(rowText)) {
+      return { kind: "footer", reason: "Copyright/footer text" };
+    }
+
+    if (STUDENT_INFO_PATTERN.test(rowText)) {
+      return { kind: "student_information", reason: "Student information" };
+    }
+
+    if (ACADEMIC_YEAR_PATTERN.test(rowText)) {
+      return { kind: "academic_year", reason: "Academic year" };
+    }
+
+    if (DATE_PATTERN.test(rowText)) {
+      return { kind: "date", reason: "Report date" };
+    }
+
+    if (DEPARTMENT_PATTERN.test(rowText)) {
+      return { kind: "department", reason: "Department/faculty heading" };
+    }
+
+    if (SUMMARY_PATTERN.test(rowText)) {
+      return { kind: "page_furniture", reason: "Aggregate/summary row" };
+    }
+
+    if (NOTE_PATTERN.test(rowText)) {
+      return { kind: "page_furniture", reason: "Report note" };
+    }
+
+    if (PAGE_FURNITURE_PATTERN.test(rowText)) {
+      return { kind: "page_furniture", reason: "Page furniture" };
+    }
+  }
+
+  // The table header row itself may contain labels that were not in
+  // HEADER_TOKENS (for example "S.No." or "Attendance").
+  if (headerY !== undefined && Math.abs(row.y - headerY) < 4) {
+    return { kind: "table_header", reason: "Table header row" };
+  }
+
+  // Rows above the table header on any page are page-level metadata. A row
+  // with a complete course shape is kept as a course candidate, so a
+  // mis-bucketed real course is never dismissed as metadata.
+  if (
+    headerY !== undefined &&
+    row.y > headerY + 4 &&
+    !hasMinimumCourseFields(row)
+  ) {
+    return { kind: "page_header", reason: "Page header above the course table" };
+  }
+
+  return null;
+}
+
+function hasMinimumCourseFields(row: RowInput): boolean {
+  const { cells } = row;
+  const rawCode = cells.code;
+  const hasCode =
+    rawCode !== undefined &&
+    rawCode !== "" &&
+    !/^\d+$/.test(rawCode.trim());
+  return (
+    hasCode &&
+    Boolean(cells.credits) &&
+    cells.thPresent !== undefined &&
+    cells.thHeld !== undefined
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * Pure row classification used by the parser.
+ *
+ * Returns `null` only when the row has the minimum fields needed to attempt a
+ * course. Metadata and incomplete rows are always accounted for explicitly.
+ */
+export function classifyRow(
+  row: RowInput,
+  headerRowYByPage: ReadonlyMap<number, number>,
+): RowClassificationResult | null {
+  const { cells } = row;
+  const rowText = Object.values(cells)
+    .filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value))
+    .join(" ")
+    .trim();
+
+  const metadata = classifyMetadataRow(row, rowText, headerRowYByPage);
+  if (metadata) {
+    return {
+      classification: "metadata",
+      code: cells.code ?? null,
+      reason: metadata.reason,
+      metadataKind: metadata.kind,
+    };
+  }
+
+  const rawCode = cells.code;
+  const hasCode =
+    rawCode !== undefined &&
+    rawCode !== "" &&
+    !/^\d+$/.test(rawCode.trim());
+  const hasCredits = Boolean(cells.credits);
+
+  if (!hasCode && !hasCredits) {
+    return {
+      classification: "incomplete",
+      code: rawCode ?? null,
+      reason: "No recognizable course code or credit-hours value",
+    };
+  }
+
+  const missingRequired: string[] = [];
+  if (!hasCode) missingRequired.push("code");
+  if (!hasCredits) missingRequired.push("credits");
+  if (cells.thPresent === undefined) missingRequired.push("thPresent");
+  if (cells.thHeld === undefined) missingRequired.push("thHeld");
+
+  if (missingRequired.length > 0) {
+    return {
+      classification: "incomplete",
+      code: rawCode ?? null,
+      reason: `Missing required fields: ${missingRequired.join(", ")}`,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return null;
+}

   }
   return null;

-/** Parse "3 Th + 1 Pr" -> { th: 3, pr: 1 }. Also handles "2 Th + 0 Pr". */
+/** Detect column anchors from header row text items. */
+function detectColumnAnchors(
+  headerRowItems: { text: string; x: number }[],
+): ColumnAnchor[] | null {
+  const anchors: ColumnAnchor[] = [];
+  const usedX = new Set<number>();
+
+  for (const item of headerRowItems) {
+    const lower = item.text.toLowerCase();
+    let matchedName: ColName | null = null;
+
+    for (const [colName, tokens] of Object.entries(HEADER_TOKENS)) {
+      if (tokens.some((t) => lower === t)) {
+        matchedName = colName as ColName;
+        break;
+      }
+    }
+
+    if (!matchedName || usedX.has(item.x)) continue;
+
+    anchors.push({ name: matchedName, x: item.x });
+    usedX.add(item.x);
+  }
+
+  // Require at least the code column anchor to consider detection successful.
+  if (!anchors.some((a) => a.name === "code")) return null;
+
+  return anchors;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Build column ranges from detected anchors, using DEFAULT_COL widths.
+ * Detected anchor x becomes the centre; DEFAULT_COL half-width is preserved
+ * so data items at slightly different x positions are not excluded.
+ * Returns null if no code anchor was detected (detection failed).
+ */
+function buildColRangesFromAnchors(
+  anchors: ColumnAnchor[],
+): Record<ColName, { min: number; max: number }> | null {
+  if (!anchors.some((a) => a.name === "code")) return null;
+
+  const ranges: Record<ColName, { min: number; max: number }> = {} as Record<ColName, { min: number; max: number }>;
+  for (const [name, defRange] of Object.entries(DEFAULT_COL)) {
+    const colName = name as ColName;
+    ranges[colName] = { ...defRange };
+  }
+
+  for (const anchor of anchors) {
+    const defRange = DEFAULT_COL[anchor.name];
+    const halfWidth = (defRange.max - defRange.min) / 2;
+    ranges[anchor.name] = {
+      min: anchor.x - halfWidth,
+      max: anchor.x + halfWidth,
+    };
+  }
+
+  return ranges;
+}
+
+/** Parse "3 Th + 1 Pr" -> { th: 3, pr: 1 }. Supports multiple credit formats. */
 function parseCredits(raw: string): { th: number; pr: number } {
-  const m = raw.match(/^(\d+)\s*Th\s*\+\s*(\d+)\s*Pr$/i);
-  if (!m) throw new Error(`Unrecognised credit-hours format: "${raw}"`);
-  return { th: parseInt(m[1], 10), pr: parseInt(m[2], 10) };
+  const patterns: RegExp[] = [
+    /^(\d+)\s*Th\s*\+\s*(\d+)\s*Pr$/i,        // "3 Th + 1 Pr"
+    /^(\d+)\s*-\s*(\d+)$/i,                    // "3-1"
+    /^(\d+)\s*T\s*\+\s*(\d+)\s*P$/i,           // "3T+1P"
+    /^(\d+)\s*\+\s*(\d+)$/i,                   // "3+1"
+    /^(\d+)\s*Th\s*(\d+)\s*Pr$/i,              // "3 Th 1 Pr"
+  ];
+
+  for (const pattern of patterns) {
+    const m = raw.match(pattern);
+    if (m) {
+      return { th: parseInt(m[1], 10), pr: parseInt(m[2], 10) };
+    }
+  }
+
+  throw new Error(`Unrecognised credit-hours format: "${raw}"`);
 }
 
-/** Parse a present/held cell: "-" -> 0, otherwise parseInt. */
-function parseCount(raw: string): number {
+/**
+ * Parse a present/held cell.
+ * Distinguishes explicit, defaulted, and missing values.
+ * - "explicit": a real digit was parsed (e.g. "18", "0")
+ * - "defaulted": a recognised missing marker (e.g. "-") -> 0
+ * - "missing": cell was absent entirely -> 0, flagged as missing
+ * Unrecognised strings throw; they are never silently converted to zero.
+ */
+function parseCount(raw: string | undefined): ParsedCount {
+  if (raw === undefined) {
+    return { value: 0, source: "missing" };
+  }
+
   const s = raw.trim();
-  if (s === "-") return 0;
+
+  // Explicit numeric value (including zero)
   const n = parseInt(s, 10);
-  if (isNaN(n)) throw new Error(`Expected a number or "-", got: "${s}"`);
-  return n;
+  if (!isNaN(n) && String(n) === s) {
+    return { value: n, source: "explicit" };
+  }
+
+  // Known missing-value markers for NED format -> defaulted to zero
+  const missingMarkers = ["-", "", "N/A", "NA", "null", "nil"];
+  if (missingMarkers.includes(s)) {
+    return { value: 0, source: "defaulted" };
+  }
+
+  // Unreadable / unrecognized value — do not silently convert to zero
+  throw new Error(`Expected a number or missing marker, got: "${s}"`);
 }
